complete 185-test regression gate passed in 12.181 seconds.

## E2.5: distributed sampler resume

E2.5 extends the production representation loop with explicit PyTorch
`DistributedSampler` state. Checkpoints record sampler type, rank, replica
count, seed, shuffle and drop-last policies, completed epoch, and shard cursor.
Restore validates the full topology before training and fails closed when a
checkpoint is opened under another rank or worker plan.

The qualification simulated two ranks over four shuffled epochs. Rank shards
were disjoint and their union covered all eight samples in every epoch. Each
rank was independently interrupted after epoch 2, restored into fresh model,
Adam, scheduler, and RNG objects, and matched its uninterrupted epoch-4 state
exactly. Restoring either checkpoint under the other rank was rejected. This
qualifies sampler state and deterministic sharding, not multi-process gradient
synchronization or throughput. E2 remains partial only for local history
export/dashboard. The complete 186-test regression gate passed in 14.054
seconds.

## E2.6: deterministic local history export

E2.6 keeps the append-only `MetricLedger` JSONL as the sole source of truth and
adds a deterministic projection into structured `history.json`, flat
`events.csv`, and a standalone `dashboard.html`. The dashboard embeds the
validated summary and filters by metric, split, and namespace without a live
tracking service. Existing output directories are accepted only when every
byte matches a fresh export.

The qualification exported three events spanning two attempts, exploratory
and selection namespaces, and three metric series. Re-export produced the same
SHA-256 hashes for all three artifacts. The JSON retained attempt boundaries,
epoch/global-step coordinates, sample counts, and namespaces. This is local
observability evidence, not a predictive-performance result. With explicit
refinement and representation checkpoints, exact local and per-rank resume,
complete histories, and deterministic local export now qualified, the E2 gate
is complete. The complete 187-test regression gate passed in 15.641 seconds.

## E3: fingerprinted model bundles

E3 adds a strict package boundary around fitted model state. Each immutable,
content-addressed bundle records the exact path, SHA-256 digest, and size of
every opaque component, then binds those identities to serialized role
fingerprints, empirical support profiles, class-column order, transform
identity, graph edges, routing authorization, semantic-cache version,
training/evaluation/metric evidence, software/environment compatibility,
creation provenance, and parent lineage. Unknown schema fields, stale support,
dimension mismatch, graph cycles, missing or extra components, and changed
bytes all fail closed.

The qualification published stable original `109ca4ee8eba801fe0eb`, replacement
`d0a372ce889c2ac04e3a`, and coordinated-expansion
`b338cdbb1af19a6c6719` identities. A same-role replacement passed. Task, input
source, output type, class-set, and transform mismatches were rejected. A class
expansion with stale downstream width was rejected, while the coordinated
three-column graph passed. Corruption prevented activation and left `CURRENT`
on the prior verified bundle. Parent rollback restored the original bundle
without rebuilding it. This is lifecycle correctness evidence, not a
predictive-performance claim. The complete 192-test regression gate passed in
14.956 seconds.

## E4: end-to-end CIFAR qualification

E4 freezes a five-seed CIFAR-100 coarse-class lifecycle over source-index-
verified MobileNetV2 features. The official CIFAR-100 test rows remain fixed
and observational, while only official-train rows enter stratified geometry,
readout-calibration, risk-control, and validation budgets. CIFAR-10 is the
near-OOD family and SVHN is the far-OOD family; policy-selection, threshold,
and final subsets are pairwise disjoint. Cache labels and reconstructed source
indices are checked against raw CIFAR arrays before fitting. The controlled
pretraining record identifies ImageNet-1K MobileNetV2 and its overlap risk.

The non-inferiority margin was frozen at 5 balanced-accuracy points from the
separate seed-42 Tier 5 pilot. Across seeds `[11, 23, 37, 53, 71]`, GEODE
multinomial reached 65.26% mean balanced accuracy, compared with 67.33% for
logistic regression and 68.13% for RBF SVM. GEODE-minus-logistic averaged
-2.07 points and its paired one-sided 95% lower bound was -2.43 points, so the
preregistered non-inferiority decision passed. This does not establish a
predictive advantage: both controls were more accurate, and logistic NLL
remained lower at 1.002 versus 1.095 for GEODE.

Maximum probability won validation-only selection for all five seeds. Its
final near/far AUROC was 0.642/0.805, AUPR-Out was 0.441/0.732, and FPR95 was
0.808/0.608. At the risk-control threshold retaining about 90% known coverage,
near/far true-positive rate was only 0.169/0.439. Generalized OOD evaluation is
therefore complete, but near-OOD rejection remains a material limitation.

The real-data recovery drill interrupted class 10, exposed a partial stage,
and resumed with zero feature reloads. Recovered validation metrics and
assembly hashes matched the uninterrupted run, and final-test rows stayed
unopened. A fresh seed-11 replay exactly matched model and prediction hashes,
classification and OOD metrics, selected score, and threshold. Candidate
bundle `18ab33416dd20c4c37d0` verifies explicit geometry, PCA/LDA/scaler,
multinomial readout, support profile, frozen config, and evaluation evidence.
This closes E4 as a lifecycle and non-inferiority result, not a state-of-the-art
classification or OOD claim. The complete 196-test regression gate passed in
14.873 seconds.

## E10: production rehearsal

E10 adds a model-format-independent replicated service around the immutable
bundle registry. An injected loader creates one predictor per replica; only the
active production bundle controls responses, while candidate predictions and
latencies are recorded in shadow. The service refuses fewer than two replicas
for this qualification. A durable, atomically replaced promotion journal
records the direct parent and candidate before the `CURRENT` pointer moves, so
a replacement coordinator can fail closed to the parent after interruption.

The canonical rehearsal served parent bundle `377ad7127e3238eea88d` on two
replicas. Inverted child `24c445b170370858dd36` had 0.0 shadow agreement and
never controlled output. Its forced failed-canary path restored the parent in
0.005916 seconds. The coordinator-loss drill activated direct child
`3e5ba13abf9428f703ea`, discarded the coordinator before completion, and
recovered through a fresh coordinator in 0.002797 seconds. Both measured
durations are below the frozen one-second RTO; RPO is zero accepted requests,
the journal ends stable, both replicas reload the parent, and no `.partial`
artifact remains. This establishes local lifecycle recovery, not multi-host
availability; E7's two-node Ray gate remains separately blocked.

## E11: final public study

E11 freezes a manifest of 27 configuration, acquisition, and result files from
E1-E10 and records their byte sizes, schemas, and SHA-256 identities. E0 has no
standalone result artifact, so its completed contract/test evidence is stated
in the milestone-status map rather than represented by a fabricated file. E7
is locked as `local_small_complete_multihost_blocked`; this expected partial state must remain
true for the publication gate to pass.

The artifact-only generator verifies the lock before producing deterministic
JSON and Markdown principal tables, a cost report, and two SVG plots. Two
independent temporary output directories had identical hashes for all five
files. The principal table reproduces five-seed CIFAR-100 balanced accuracy of
65.26% for GEODE, 67.33% for logistic regression, and 68.13% for RBF SVM.
Near/far OOD FPR95 remains 0.8081/0.6082. Recorded E4 wall time totals 95.521
seconds across the five local runs; this is host-specific cost evidence, not a
normalized benchmark. No dataset is opened and no model is fitted by the E11
command.

## M16: v5 protocol and factorial harness

M16 adds the research contract required by the frozen-space program without
changing production model behavior. `v5_protocol.py` defines S0-S5 stages,
frozen seed lists, split-label use boundaries, representation lineage, explicit
gate operands, and declarative protocol validation. `v5_registry.py` expands
representation/head/readout cells deterministically and rejects missing controls
or unequal split hashes. `v5_artifacts.py` validates canonical run and migration
records, enforces representation-hash compatibility, counts parameters and
serialized bytes, and builds SHA-256 indexes. `v5_statistics.py` supplies paired
prediction intervals and the six-axis-compatible Pareto dominance primitive.

The S0 command crossed identity and fixed-affine representations with linear,
RBF, prototype, Gaussian-mixture, and current-GEODE heads. It generated ten run
records twice with byte-identical files, rejected a deliberate split mismatch,
and indexed the final evidence under `logs/results/v5/m16_s0/`. The metrics
payload hash is
`44a4a1d29d6ee3d5878cb8590969a3100ba9aaa0e529144530d0bc91c52e5460`.
This is protocol evidence only: the toy accuracies do not support a predictive
claim. Fifteen focused tests passed, and the complete repository gate now
contains 228 tests.

## M18: low-rank, shared metrics, and support policy

M18 introduces one factorized precision contract for spherical, diagonal, full,
diagonal-plus-low-rank, shared-full-plus-diagonal, and
shared-low-rank-plus-diagonal metrics. Every family enforces an explicit
eigenvalue floor, supports deterministic JSON replay, exposes exact
factorized/dense quadratic-score parity and analytic point gradients, and can be
converted to the existing ellipsoid representation. Local metric fitting is an
opt-in `GreedyConstructor` path; defaults and existing call sites remain
unchanged.

The frozen synthetic study contains 1,920 development and 1,920 unseen test
records over seeds 11, 23, and 37. It varies ambient dimension, known intrinsic
rank, observations per class, condition number, contamination, and class
separation. Development chose `shared_full_diagonal` globally and for four of
five support bins; the medium-support/high-rank bin chose
`diagonal_low_rank:rank=0`.

The adaptive policy did not transfer. On unseen cells, aggregate loss was
0.34797 versus 0.34244 for the frozen `shared_full_diagonal` control. Balanced
accuracy was 86.859% versus 87.183% (-0.323 pp), and NLL was 0.31512 versus
0.31040. Both used median 540 parameter bytes and 9,996 deterministic fit-work
units, so neither resource measure improved. Predictive and resource gates both failed. This is a stopped
negative branch: the crossover map is retained, the new families remain opt-in,
and no M18 policy becomes a default or an input to later claims.

## Epoch histories

Tier 6 supervised refinement returns `epoch_history`, with one record per epoch:

- `iteration` and `epoch`;
- `batch_training_loss`;
- full sampled `train_loss` and `train_error` after the epoch;
- observational `test_loss` and `test_error` after the epoch;
- `monitor_used_for_learning: false`.

The test monitor is excluded from gradients, minibatch ordering, scale updates,
early stopping, and hyperparameter selection. Production Tier 6 currently uses
zero refinement epochs, so histories are empty unless refinement is explicitly
enabled.

## Next update

Current through M15.2b (2026-07-25). M7-M15 are complete. The per-class
temperature gate failed, so mixture weights and combined likelihood variants
remain blocked. Global covariance temperature is the retained M15 probability
adapter. Calibrated open-set
inference remains experimental because no M10 cell passed the frozen production
gate. M11 now reliably accumulates recurring unsupported events for review,
keeps semantic assignment human-gated, and publishes no model mutation.

The retained automatic-grouping reference is the M11.29 complete-buffer
HDBSCAN policy in normalized MobileNet space: 100% accumulated event recall,
61.1% distinct-group recall, 0.059 noise-inclusive adjusted Rand index, and
66.8% duplicate review rate on untouched classes 8/9. Later isolated controls
did not pass the full advancement gate. Streaming memory reduced review burden
but collapsed distinct recovery; silhouette-selected K-means improved adjusted
Rand index to 0.449 but reduced distinct-group recall to 38.9% and increased
duplicates; joint unlabeled PCA reconstruction reduced distinct-group recall
to 16.7%. A metric learned from delayed proxy-review pair constraints preserved
61.1% distinct recovery and improved adjusted Rand index to 0.082, but increased
duplicate reviews to 72.6%. These methods remain analysis or provenance
controls and do not replace the retained partition policy or source
representation.

M12 closes with a negative exit gate. Exhaustive exact routing remains the only
bound route; candidate routers and compression remain experimental and unbound.
No real-feature confirmation is warranted because no alternative passed its
complete toy quality-and-resource advancement gate.
M13 retains the sphere as the leading Tier 4 primitive candidate under the
matched GPU protocol, but does not replace ellipsoids globally. Validation-
gated subtraction remains disabled because it was null for sphere/diagonal and
slightly negative for full covariance.
Final labels stay observational. Automatic semantic IDs, class creation,
production representation updates, real model mutation, and LLM semantic
proposals remain disabled.

E4 closes the CIFAR qualification with disjoint raw-source-indexed budgets,
five confirmatory seeds, matched controls, generalized near/far OOD, exact
replay and interruption recovery, and a verified candidate bundle. The result
passes non-inferiority but remains behind logistic/RBF controls and exposes
weak near-OOD rejection. M15 does not change the end-to-end stage order:
global covariance temperature remains an optional calibration-fold readout.
E5 closes with a negative candidate-promotion result. The deployed E4 bundle
was reconstructed exactly, then four candidate routers were measured in shadow
at batch sizes 1, 32, 256, and 1,024. Every run reported exhaustive agreement,
work counters, fallback, and latency while exhaustive outputs remained
authoritative. Agreement ranged from 95.3% to 100%; the best bound result still
evaluated 94.0% of exhaustive class pairs and the certified top-5 route fell
back on every sample. No candidate preserved deployed normalized winning scores
or improved p95 latency, and E4's multinomial readout requires the complete
class-score vector. All candidates therefore remain blocked. E6 transfer-
learning qualification is next.

E6 qualifies a parent-linked CIFAR-100-to-CIFAR-10 proxy transfer on the
controlled MobileNetV2 representation. The GEODE head reached 81.96% balanced
accuracy and passed the frozen target-improvement gate, with exact zero source
forgetting and byte-identical replay of transform, geometry, readout, and
predictions. It remained behind the raw linear probe (82.71%) and supervised
adapter probe (82.97%), so the result demonstrates lifecycle-safe transfer but
not target-task leadership. Child bundle `aaa5a73c0b813240876d` points to E4
parent `18ab33416dd20c4c37d0` without replacing the current pointer. The
external-pretraining lane is separately reported as not run because no second
frozen checkpoint exists. E7 DomainNet distributed qualification is next.

E7's final gate is blocked on physical multi-host execution, not data. The pinned public
`wltjr1007/DomainNet` revision
`ee20570ae7a29c51571e55a9a17983f7625295d6` is cached outside the repository at
`D:\geode-ml\data\cache`: four Parquet files, 18,521,436,207 verified bytes,
586,575 declared samples, 345 classes, and all six canonical domains. The
schema-v2 manifest separates those physical mixed-domain files from logical
domain metadata while retaining schema-v1 compatibility. The canonical
preflight preserves disjoint clipart validation/sketch final-domain selection.
Python 3.12.10 Ray Client connects to a Docker cluster with three logical Ray
2.56.1 nodes. A bounded real-data episode decoded 192 images spanning eight
classes and all six domains through 49 row-group tasks; work executed on every
node and two feature passes produced identical SHA-256. Its source-only
nearest-centroid control reached 18.75% clipart validation and 28.125%
observational sketch balanced accuracy. A separate forced worker-process loss
recovered through Ray retry with exact complete histories. These are local
systems checks on one physical host, not cluster-small flagship evidence. E7
still awaits distinct physical nodes and physical node-loss recovery.

E8 packages real WikiText-103 and point-cloud GEODE models under one immutable
JSON/NumPy bundle contract, and both replay exact prediction hashes after load.
The 32-character text head reached 24.47% top-1 and 58.33% top-5 over 93.6% of
held-out samples, trailing the matched linear (33.92%) and 3-gram (38.57%)
controls. The pinned ModelNet40 source provides 12,308 verified shapes, 40
labels, and 2,048 points per shape. On the bounded eight-shape episode, 29
GEODE experts improved mean absolute SDF residual from 0.2873 for one sphere to
0.2767. Bundle `d0c93fb69431fb8bab7a` replays both modalities exactly and the
E8 gate passes. The earlier ModelNet10 proxy bundle remains in the append-only
registry for provenance but is no longer current.

E9 closes the review-to-publication lifecycle with a persistent review ID and
a delayed confirmation linked before mutation. A deliberately excessive
calibration NLL increase was rejected with no pointer change; the passing
candidate then completed a nonpublishing dry run and all replay, calibration,
graph, and final-label-isolation gates. Confirmed child bundle
`a151b8f3c1cfa763f677` records parent `b5e3f04cf40c9d321a82`. Exact rollback
restored that parent as current while retaining both immutable bundles and the
full rejected, dry-run, published, and rolled-back transaction records.

E10 closes the local production-rehearsal lifecycle. Two replicas served exact
parent `377ad7127e3238eea88d`; the bad shadow canary never controlled output and
both failed-canary and coordinator-loss faults restored that parent under the
one-second RTO with zero-request RPO. E7 still requires a genuine second Ray
node.

E11 closes the final public-study publication gate. Twenty-seven frozen inputs
are SHA-256 locked, and one command regenerates five byte-identical outputs
without training. E7 remains the sole incomplete end-to-end milestone because
its gate requires a genuine second Ray node.
